- **owflow:migration-fix**: Migration — conditional verification-issue resolution with a max-3 fix-then-reverify loop and a data-integrity HALT that is never auto-fixed (issues-resolved). ([palucdev/owflow](https://github.com/palucdev/owflow/tree/HEAD/src/skills/migration-fix) · ★4)
- **owflow:migration-implement**: Migration — executes the approved implementation plan via isolated per-group delegation (normal lane only), producing the work log and the migration-executed slug. ([palucdev/owflow](https://github.com/palucdev/owflow/tree/HEAD/src/skills/migration-implement) · ★4)

- **owflow:migration-target**: Migration — plans the target state and gap inventory, classifies the migration type, recommends a strategy, and records external research. - **owflow:performance-spec**: Performance — requirements gathering, specification creation via delegation, diagram refinement, and conditional specification audit. Continues with performance-plan. ([palucdev/owflow](https://github.com/palucdev/owflow/tree/HEAD/src/skills/performance-spec) · ★4)
- **owflow:performance-verify**: Performance — verification options prompt, comprehensive implementation verification, and user-driven fix loop. Continues with performance-finalize. ([palucdev/owflow](https://github.com/palucdev/owflow/tree/HEAD/src/skills/performance-verify) · ★4)

- **owflow:research-converge**: Research skill — optional subskill. Never a combined summary-table confirm. ([palucdev/owflow](https://github.com/palucdev/owflow/tree/HEAD/src/skills/research-converge) · ★4)
- **owflow:research-design**: Research skill — optional subskill. - **owflow:research-finalize**: Research skill — terminal step. Inventories all research outputs (research report always; brainstorm/design artifacts conditionally), presents the workflow results box, and gets user confirmation that the research is correct. Marks the task research-completed — the Exit-Gate acceptance here is where the goal-research wrapper's loop ends. ([palucdev/owflow](https://github.com/palucdev/owflow/tree/HEAD/src/skills/research-finalize) · ★4)
- **owflow:research-fork**: Research utility — forks a research task by copying its directory wholesale into a NEW task, then trimming the copied state so the fork continues from a chosen completed step (fork point) onwards. Passing only the task path is enough — the skill orients on the source research, then asks the user (with suggestions) for the fork's intent, fork point, and name. The source task is never modified. ([palucdev/owflow](https://github.com/palucdev/owflow/tree/HEAD/src/skills/research-fork) · ★4)
- **owflow:research-gather**: Research skill — reads the plan's Gathering Strategy and launches the parallel information-gatherer agent fan in ONE message, then merges per-category findings. - **owflow:research-plan**: Research skill — creates the research brief, delegates methodology selection and planning to the research-planner agent, and writes the research plan with a parsable Gathering Strategy. - **owflow:research-quick**: Research skill — the quick lane. Produces the same standard artifacts and state as the full lane; continuable by any research subskill at full fidelity. ([palucdev/owflow](https://github.com/palucdev/owflow/tree/HEAD/src/skills/research-quick) · ★4)
- **owflow:research-synthesize**: Research skill — delegates synthesis and evidence-based reporting to the research-synthesizer agent, producing the synthesis document and the comprehensive research report with per-finding confidence. ([palucdev/owflow](https://github.com/palucdev/owflow/tree/HEAD/src/skills/research-synthesize) · ★4)
